{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Fzu3x8ys7WAeXMrZyVdPA7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport {\n2\t  pinSearchPath,\n3\t  SourceOnlyMigration,\n4\t} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/database';\n5\timport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n6\t\n7\t/**\n8\t * Create the auth-service transactional outbox table (DATA-HIGH-001).\n9\t *\n10\t * Mirrors the messaging_outbox contract: a single source-owned table in the\n11\t * `auth` schema that every state-change writer INSERTs into within the same\n12\t * transaction as its domain write, so the domain row and the outbox row commit\n13\t * atomically (no dual-write event loss between commit and NATS publish). The\n14\t * OutboxWorkerService then publishes asynchronously with at-least-once + retry\n15\t * + dead-letter semantics.\n16\t *\n17\t * Columns mirror OutboxEntityBase exactly (the AuthOutbox entity is\n18\t * synchronize:false; this migration is the schema owner).\n19\t */\n20\t@SourceOnlyMigration({\n21\t  reason:\n22\t    'auth_outbox is source-owned infrastructure and must never be cloned into tenant schemas',\n23\t})\n24\texport class CreateAuthOutboxTable1800600000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n25\t  name = 'CreateAuthOutboxTable1800600000000';\n26\t\n27\t  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n28\t    await pinSearchPath(queryRunner, 'auth');\n29\t\n30\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n31\t      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth.auth_outbox (\n32\t        \"id\" UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n33\t        \"eventType\" VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,\n34\t        \"tenantId\" UUID NULL,\n35\t        \"aggregateId\" UUID NULL,\n36\t        \"payload\" JSONB NOT NULL,\n37\t        \"createdAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n38\t        \"publishedAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n39\t        \"retryCount\" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n40\t        \"lastError\" TEXT NULL,\n41\t        \"nextAttemptAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n42\t        \"idempotencyKey\" VARCHAR(255) NULL,\n43\t        \"isDeadLettered\" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\n44\t        \"leasedAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n45\t        \"leasedBy\" VARCHAR(128) NULL\n46\t      )\n47\t    `);\n48\t\n49\t    // Poll predicate index: the worker scans unpublished, non-dead-lettered\n50\t    // rows oldest-first. Partial index keeps it small as published rows pile up.\n51\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n52\t      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"idx_auth_outbox_poll\"\n53\t        ON auth.auth_outbox (\"createdAt\")\n54\t        WHERE \"publishedAt\" IS NULL AND \"isDeadLettered\" = false\n55\t    `);\n56\t\n57\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n58\t      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"idx_auth_outbox_tenant\"\n59\t        ON auth.auth_outbox (\"tenantId\")\n60\t    `);\n61\t\n62\t    // Idempotency: at most one outbox row per (tenant, idempotencyKey) so a\n63\t    // retried command cannot enqueue the same event twice.\n64\t    await queryRunner.query(`\n65\t      CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"idx_auth_outbox_idempotency\"\n66\t        ON auth.auth_outbox (\"tenantId\", \"idempotencyKey\")\n67\t        WHERE \"idempotencyKey\" IS NOT NULL\n68\t    `);\n69\t  }\n70\t\n71\t  public async down(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n72\t    await pinSearchPath(queryRunner, 'auth');\n73\t    await queryRunner.query(`DROP INDEX IF EXISTS auth.\"idx_auth_outbox_idempotency\"`);\n74\t    await queryRunner.query(`DROP INDEX IF EXISTS auth.\"idx_auth_outbox_tenant\"`);\n75\t    await queryRunner.query(`DROP INDEX IF EXISTS auth.\"idx_auth_outbox_poll\"`);\n76\t    await queryRunner.query(`DROP TABLE IF EXISTS auth.auth_outbox`);\n77\t  }\n78\t}\n79\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8070c131-7617-49fa-b7e0-d0733efc07c0","uuid":"52e3eaa5-861c-478f-961c-e41c4217acbd","timestamp":"2026-10-08T01:50:33.338Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-bacb8bed08ee/apps/auth-service/src/migrations/1800600000000-CreateAuthOutboxTable.ts","content":"import {\n  pinSearchPath,\n  SourceOnlyMigration,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/database';\nimport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n\n/**\n * Create the auth-service transactional outbox table (DATA-HIGH-001).\n *\n * Mirrors the messaging_outbox contract: a single source-owned table in the\n * `auth` schema that every state-change writer INSERTs into within the same\n * transaction as its domain write, so the domain row and the outbox row commit\n * atomically (no dual-write event loss between commit and NATS publish). The\n * OutboxWorkerService then publishes asynchronously with at-least-once + retry\n * + dead-letter semantics.\n *\n * Columns mirror OutboxEntityBase exactly (the AuthOutbox entity is\n * synchronize:false; this migration is the schema owner).\n */\n@SourceOnlyMigration({\n  reason:\n    'auth_outbox is source-owned infrastructure and must never be cloned into tenant schemas',\n})\nexport class CreateAuthOutboxTable1800600000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n  name = 'CreateAuthOutboxTable1800600000000';\n\n  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    await pinSearchPath(queryRunner, 'auth');\n\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth.auth_outbox (\n        \"id\" UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n        \"eventType\" VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,\n        \"tenantId\" UUID NULL,\n        \"aggregateId\" UUID NULL,\n        \"payload\" JSONB NOT NULL,\n        \"createdAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n        \"publishedAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n        \"retryCount\" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n        \"lastError\" TEXT NULL,\n        \"nextAttemptAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n        \"idempotencyKey\" VARCHAR(255) NULL,\n        \"isDeadLettered\" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\n        \"leasedAt\" TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,\n        \"leasedBy\" VARCHAR(128) NULL\n      )\n    `);\n\n    // Poll predicate index: the worker scans unpublished, non-dead-lettered\n    // rows oldest-first. Partial index keeps it small as published rows pile up.\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"idx_auth_outbox_poll\"\n        ON auth.auth_outbox (\"createdAt\")\n        WHERE \"publishedAt\" IS NULL AND \"isDeadLettered\" = false\n    `);\n\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"idx_auth_outbox_tenant\"\n        ON auth.auth_outbox (\"tenantId\")\n    `);\n\n    // Idempotency: at most one outbox row per (tenant, idempotencyKey) so a\n    // retried command cannot enqueue the same event twice.\n    await queryRunner.query(`\n      CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS \"idx_auth_outbox_idempotency\"\n        ON auth.auth_outbox (\"tenantId\", \"idempotencyKey\")\n        WHERE \"idempotencyKey\" IS NOT NULL\n    `);\n  }\n\n  public async down(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    await pinSearchPath(queryRunner, 'auth');\n    await queryRunner.query(`DROP INDEX IF EXISTS auth.\"idx_auth_outbox_idempotency\"`);\n    await queryRunner.query(`DROP INDEX IF EXISTS auth.\"idx_auth_outbox_tenant\"`);\n    await queryRunner.query(`DROP INDEX IF EXISTS auth.\"idx_auth_outbox_poll\"`);\n    await queryRunner.query(`DROP TABLE IF EXISTS auth.auth_outbox`);\n  }\n}\n","numLines":79,"startLine":1,"totalLines":79}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HXxg8h2LE9UPME8hQmFYKe","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Schema Invariants\n3\t * ============================================================================\n4\t *\n5\t * Asserts the architectural invariants established by the 2026-04-14\n6\t * public-schema teardown plan (docs/plans/2026-04-14-...). These are\n7\t * static facts about the database — no service interactions, no tenant\n8\t * fixtures, just SQL against the live test DB.\n9\t *\n10\t * # Why this test exists\n11\t *\n12\t * Before the teardown, services silently leaked tables into `public`\n13\t * because @Entity decorators omitted the `schema:` option. The drift\n14\t * went undetected until services failed to bootstrap RLS and a\n15\t * production multi-tenant isolation hole opened. A CI invariant on the\n16\t * physical schema layout makes that class of regression impossible by\n17\t * construction — any new entity that forgets `schema:` lands in public,\n18\t * the test below fails, the build is rejected.\n19\t *\n20\t * # What it checks\n21\t *\n22\t *   1. `public` schema contains ONLY the migrations meta + PostgreSQL\n23\t *      extension artifacts. Zero application tables.\n24\t *\n25\t *   2. The `shared` schema contains exactly the four genuinely\n26\t *      cross-service tables (audit_logs, gdpr_data_requests,\n27\t *      user_consents, access_logs).\n28\t *\n29\t *   3. Every table that was moved during P6-P9 lives in its expected\n30\t *      owning schema (and NOT in public).\n31\t *\n32\t * # When this test fails\n33\t *\n34\t *   - A new entity was added without `schema:` → relocate the entity\n35\t *     by adding the missing decorator option.\n36\t *   - A migration moved a table to public for a transition phase →\n37\t *     either complete the move forward in the same PR or update this\n38\t *     test's allowed-list with a justification comment.\n39\t *   - The shared schema acquired or lost a table → either codify the\n40\t *     change in this allow-list (with PR review on the change) or\n41\t *     revert the migration.\n42\t */\n43\t\n44\timport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'fs';\n45\timport { createRequire } from 'module';\n46\timport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n47\t\n48\timport { PROTECTED_TABLES } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/constants';\n49\timport { MODULE_SCHEMAS } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/database';\n50\timport { getMetadataArgsStorage } from 'typeorm';\n51\t\n52\timport { TestDatabase } from '../../helpers/db.helper';\n53\t\n54\tconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..');\n55\t\n56\tconst requireEntity = createRequire(__filename);\n57\t\n58\t/**\n59\t * Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — TENANT_SCOPED vs PLATFORM_LEVEL audit lists.\n60\t *\n61\t * Hardcodes the operator's clarification (Dalga-5 brief): exactly\n62\t * which schemas spawn `tenant_<uuid>` clones vs which stay platform-\n63\t * global. The two sets together cover every owned schema in the SaaS\n64\t * topology; their union, plus `shared` + `public`, is the full\n65\t * inventory.\n66\t *\n67\t * - TENANT_SCOPED schemas MUST have at least one tenant_<uuid> clone\n68\t *   in the active test DB (assertion B.5a). The presence of clones\n69\t *   is what makes \"schema-per-tenant\" visible at the storage layer.\n70\t * - PLATFORM_LEVEL schemas MUST have ZERO tenant_<uuid>.<sametable>\n71\t *   clones (assertion B.5b). A clone of `auth.users` would be a\n72\t *   tenant-isolation hole — auth is the cross-tenant trust anchor\n73\t *   per CLAUDE.md \"tenant row placement (D14)\".\n74\t */\n75\tconst TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n76\t  'farm',\n77\t  'sensor',\n78\t  'hr',\n79\t  'messaging',\n80\t  'hydroponics',\n81\t  'ai',\n82\t  'alert',\n83\t];\n84\t\n85\tconst PLATFORM_LEVEL_SCHEMAS: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n86\t  'auth',\n87\t  'billing',\n88\t  'admin',\n89\t  'notification',\n90\t  'event_store',\n91\t  'observability',\n92\t  'config',\n93\t  'gateway',\n94\t  'shared',\n95\t  // Registry-completeness sweep (ORPHAN-HIGH-365): compliance (legal_holds,\n96\t  // admin-api-owned) + platform (db-migrate bootstrap tables) — single\n97\t  // cross-tenant schemas, never per-tenant cloned.\n98\t  'compliance',\n99\t  'platform',\n100\t];\n101\t\n102\tconst TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyArray<string>> = new Map(\n103\t  MODULE_SCHEMAS.map((moduleSchema) => [moduleSchema.sourceSchema, moduleSchema.tables]),\n104\t);\n105\t\n106\t/**\n107\t * Every table name a tenant schema is SUPPOSED to carry, from the registry.\n108\t *\n109\t * B.5b looks for platform-schema tables cloned into a tenant, and it can only\n110\t * compare by NAME — `information_schema` has no notion of \"clone of\". Names are\n111\t * not unique across schemas, so a per-tenant table whose name also exists in a\n112\t * platform schema reads as a trust-boundary violation when it is nothing of the\n113\t * kind: `messages` lives in `auth` and `admin` AND is messaging's per-tenant\n114\t * table; `retention_policies` and `legal_holds` collide the same way.\n115\t *\n116\t * That never surfaced because B.5b had never run against a database with a real\n117\t * tenant in it — B.5a was passing vacuously against zero tenants for the same\n118\t * reason. The first genuinely provisioned tenant produced four false positives\n119\t * and no true ones.\n120\t *\n121\t * Subtracting the registered per-tenant names keeps the assertion pointed at\n122\t * what it means: a tenant table that is a clone of something only a platform\n123\t * schema owns. A platform table that ALSO gets registered per-tenant would stop\n124\t * being caught here — but that registration is itself the violation, and\n125\t * `entity-schema-declaration` and B.1/B.2 are the gates that own it.\n126\t */\n127\tconst REGISTERED_TENANT_TABLE_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(\n128\t  MODULE_SCHEMAS.flatMap((moduleSchema) => [\n129\t    ...moduleSchema.tables,\n130\t    ...(moduleSchema.referenceDataTables ?? []),\n131\t  ]),\n132\t);\n133\t\n134\t// The TENANT_SCOPED set is also the allow-list for entities legitimately\n135\t// declaring `schema: undefined` (farm-pattern: SchemaManagerService\n136\t// routes them at provision time, so the @Entity decorator stays\n137\t// schema-agnostic). Any entity outside this allow-list that omits\n138\t// `schema:` is a regression — see assertion B.1.\n139\tconst TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n140\t  'farm-service',\n141\t  'sensor-service',\n142\t  'hr-service',\n143\t  'messaging-service',\n144\t  'hydroponics-service',\n145\t  'ai-service',\n146\t  'alert-engine',\n147\t]);\n148\t\n149\t/**\n150\t * Discover entity files under `apps/<service>/src` for static @Entity\n151\t * audit (B.1, B.2). Mirrors the bootstrap-from-scratch helper but lives\n152\t * separately so this spec has no cross-spec import dependency.\n153\t */\n154\tfunction discoverEntityFiles(): Array<{ service: string; file: string }> {\n155\t  const out: Array<{ service: string; file: string }> = [];\n156\t  const appsRoot = join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n157\t  if (!existsSync(appsRoot)) return out;\n158\t  for (const svc of readdirSync(appsRoot)) {\n159\t    const svcRoot = join(appsRoot, svc, 'src');\n160\t    if (!existsSync(svcRoot)) continue;\n161\t    const stack: string[] = [svcRoot];\n162\t    while (stack.length > 0) {\n163\t      const cur = stack.pop();\n164\t      if (!cur) continue;\n165\t      let entries: string[];\n166\t      try {\n167\t        entries = readdirSync(cur);\n168\t      } catch {\n169\t        continue;\n170\t      }\n171\t      for (const name of entries) {\n172\t        if (name === 'migrations' || name === '__tests__' || name === 'node_modules') continue;\n173\t        const full = join(cur, name);\n174\t        let st;\n175\t        try {\n176\t          st = statSync(full);\n177\t        } catch {\n178\t          continue;\n179\t        }\n180\t        if (st.isDirectory()) stack.push(full);\n181\t        else if (st.isFile() && /\\.entity\\.ts$/.test(name)) {\n182\t          out.push({ service: svc, file: full });\n183\t        }\n184\t      }\n185\t    }\n186\t  }\n187\t  return out;\n188\t}\n189\t\n190\t/**\n191\t * Load every @Entity registered with TypeORM by requiring each entity\n192\t * file. Duplicates protection: getMetadataArgsStorage() is a global\n193\t * singleton; we read its `tables` array AFTER all requires complete.\n194\t */\n195\tfunction loadAllEntityTableArgs(): Array<{\n196\t  serviceName: string;\n197\t  filePath: string;\n198\t  schema: string | undefined;\n199\t  tableName: string | undefined;\n200\t  targetName: string;\n201\t}> {\n202\t  const files = discoverEntityFiles();\n203\t  // Map target class -> the service+file that registered it. We\n204\t  // populate this BEFORE require() to attribute drift back to the\n205\t  // owning service when getMetadataArgsStorage() reports a target.\n206\t  const ownership = new Map<object, { service: string; file: string }>();\n207\t  for (const { service, file } of files) {\n208\t    let mod: Record<string, unknown>;\n209\t    try {\n210\t      mod = requireEntity(file) as Record<string, unknown>;\n211\t    } catch {\n212\t      continue;\n213\t    }\n214\t    for (const v of Object.values(mod)) {\n215\t      if (typeof v === 'function' && /^[A-Z]/.test(v.name) && !ownership.has(v)) {\n216\t        ownership.set(v, { service, file });\n217\t      }\n218\t    }\n219\t  }\n220\t\n221\t  const storage = getMetadataArgsStorage();\n222\t  return storage.tables.map((t) => {\n223\t    const target = typeof t.target === 'function' ? t.target : null;\n224\t    const own = target ? ownership.get(target) : undefined;\n225\t    return {\n226\t      serviceName: own?.service ?? 'unknown',\n227\t      filePath: own?.file ?? '<unknown>',\n228\t      schema: t.schema,\n229\t      tableName: typeof t.name === 'string' ? t.name : undefined,\n230\t      targetName: target?.name ?? 'unknown-target',\n231\t    };\n232\t  });\n233\t}\n234\t\n235\tconst ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES = new Set<string>([\n236\t  // TypeORM migration ledger — system table, not application data.\n237\t  'migrations',\n238\t]);\n239\t\n240\t/** One row of bootstrap stage 008's least-privilege role table. */\n241\tinterface LeastPrivilegeSpec {\n242\t  readonly schema_name: string;\n243\t  readonly owner_role: string;\n244\t  readonly runtime_role: string;\n245\t  readonly provisioner_role: string | null;\n246\t}\n247\t\n248\tconst LEAST_PRIVILEGE_STAGE = resolve(\n249\t  REPO_ROOT,\n250\t  'apps/db-migrate/src/sql/platform-bootstrap/008-least-privilege-hardening.sql',\n251\t);\n252\t\n253\t/**\n254\t * The (schema, owner role, runtime role) triples, READ FROM the bootstrap stage\n255\t * that establishes them.\n256\t *\n257\t * B.4 used to carry its own hardcoded list. It drifted — it still expected the\n258\t * pre-stage-008 model where the runtime role owned the schema — and nothing\n259\t * caught the drift because the spec was never executed (FARM-MEDIUM-303). A\n260\t * second copy of a security-relevant mapping is exactly the thing that goes\n261\t * stale silently, so this one is derived: the stage embeds its table as a JSON\n262\t * array literal fed to `jsonb_to_recordset`, which is machine-readable as-is.\n263\t *\n264\t * Parse failure THROWS at module load rather than yielding an empty list — an\n265\t * empty list would turn every B.4 case into a no-op and the gate would report\n266\t * green while asserting nothing.\n267\t */\n268\tfunction readLeastPrivilegeSpecs(): readonly LeastPrivilegeSpec[] {\n269\t  const sql = readFileSync(LEAST_PRIVILEGE_STAGE, 'utf8');\n270\t  const match = sql.match(/jsonb_to_recordset\\(\\s*'(\\[[\\s\\S]*?\\])'::jsonb/);\n271\t  if (!match?.[1]) {\n272\t    throw new Error(\n273\t      `B.4 could not read the least-privilege role table from ${LEAST_PRIVILEGE_STAGE}. ` +\n274\t        \"The stage still exists but its `jsonb_to_recordset('[...]'::jsonb)` literal no \" +\n275\t        'longer matches — update this parser rather than re-hardcoding the roles here.',\n276\t    );\n277\t  }\n278\t  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(match[1]);\n279\t  if (!Array.isArray(parsed) || parsed.length === 0) {\n280\t    throw new Error(\n281\t      `B.4 least-privilege role table parsed to an empty list — refusing to run vacuously.`,\n282\t    );\n283\t  }\n284\t  return parsed as LeastPrivilegeSpec[];\n285\t}\n286\t\n287\tconst LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS = readLeastPrivilegeSpecs();\n288\t\n289\t// ORPHAN-179: derive the shared-schema canonical table set from the\n290\t// PROTECTED_TABLES SSoT (libs/backend-common/.../protected-tables.ts) instead of\n291\t// hand-copying it (this was the 4th unguarded copy). PROTECTED_TABLES already\n292\t// carries the `shared.*` entries — audit_logs (7y), gdpr_data_requests,\n293\t// user_consents, access_logs (request-level, 90d; survives\n294\t// tenant deletion for forensics). One list, no drift.\n295\t// user_permissions retired 2026-07-12 (ADR-042, ORPHAN-HIGH-378).\n296\tconst SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES = new Set<string>(\n297\t  PROTECTED_TABLES.filter((t) => t.startsWith('shared.')).map((t) => t.slice('shared.'.length)),\n298\t);\n299\t\n300\t/**\n301\t * Tables moved out of `public` during P6-P9 of the teardown. Each entry\n302\t * is `[tableName, expectedSchema]` and is asserted both directions:\n303\t * the table EXISTS in expectedSchema and DOES NOT exist in public.\n304\t */\n305\tconst MOVED_TABLES: Array<[string, string]> = [\n306\t  // P6 — low-risk singletons\n307\t  ['channel_detection_log', 'sensor'],\n308\t  ['device_tokens', 'notification'],\n309\t  ['weather_settings', 'farm'],\n310\t  ['sentinel_hub_settings', 'farm'],\n311\t  // P7 — moderate\n312\t  ['sensor_type_definitions', 'sensor'],\n313\t  ['feeder_calibrations', 'farm'],\n314\t  ['marine_observations', 'farm'],\n315\t  ['weather_observations', 'farm'],\n316\t  ['notification_logs', 'notification'],\n317\t  // P8 — high-complexity\n318\t  ['tenant_roles', 'auth'],\n319\t  ['employees', 'hr'],\n320\t];\n321\t\n322\t/**\n323\t * Messaging service tables — established in their messaging-schema home\n324\t * by the 2026-04-14 messaging-isolation plan (P7 entity decoration).\n325\t * Asserts each of the 17 messaging entities lives in `messaging` and\n326\t * NOT in `public`. Same assertion semantics as MOVED_TABLES.\n327\t */\n328\tconst MESSAGING_TABLES: Array<[string, string]> = [\n329\t  ['channels', 'messaging'],\n330\t  ['channel_members', 'messaging'],\n331\t  ['messages', 'messaging'],\n332\t  ['message_attachments', 'messaging'],\n333\t  ['message_receipts', 'messaging'],\n334\t  ['message_reactions', 'messaging'],\n335\t  ['pinned_messages', 'messaging'],\n336\t  ['messaging_outbox', 'messaging'],\n337\t  ['retention_policies', 'messaging'],\n338\t  ['legal_holds', 'messaging'],\n339\t  ['compliance_audit_log', 'messaging'],\n340\t  ['message_analysis', 'messaging'],\n341\t  ['message_entity_references', 'messaging'],\n342\t  ['knowledge_entries', 'messaging'],\n343\t  ['embeddings_metadata', 'messaging'],\n344\t  ['user_ai_consents', 'messaging'],\n345\t];\n346\t\n347\tdescribe('Schema Invariants (2026-04-14 public-schema teardown)', () => {\n348\t  const db = new TestDatabase();\n349\t\n350\t  afterAll(async () => {\n351\t    await db.close();\n352\t  });\n353\t\n354\t  it('public schema contains no application tables', async () => {\n355\t    const result = await db.query<{ tablename: string }>(\n356\t      `SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables\n357\t       WHERE schemaname = 'public'\n358\t       ORDER BY tablename`,\n359\t    );\n360\t\n361\t    const offenders = result.rows\n362\t      .map((r) => r.tablename)\n363\t      .filter((name) => !ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES.has(name));\n364\t\n365\t    if (offenders.length > 0) {\n366\t      throw new Error(\n367\t        `public schema must contain only ${[...ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES].join(', ')}, ` +\n368\t          `but found extra tables: ${offenders.join(', ')}. ` +\n369\t          `Either move them to their owning service schema (add ` +\n370\t          `\\`schema: '<owner>'\\` to the @Entity decorator + write a ` +\n371\t          `SET SCHEMA migration), OR codify them in ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES ` +\n372\t          `with a PR review explaining why public ownership is correct.`,\n373\t      );\n374\t    }\n375\t  });\n376\t\n377\t  it('shared schema strict allow-list (B.3) — exactly the 4 canonical cross-service tables', async () => {\n378\t    // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — B.3 strict allow-list, reduced to 4 entries by\n379\t    // ADR-042 (user_permissions retired, ORPHAN-HIGH-378).\n380\t    // The 4 canonical tables are: audit_logs, gdpr_data_requests,\n381\t    // user_consents, access_logs. A 5th requires\n382\t    // an ADR per ADR-011 + architectural-arbiter approval (W5\n383\t    // `add-shared-table` skill gate — BLOCKER-15).\n384\t    const result = await db.query<{ tablename: string }>(\n385\t      `SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables\n386\t       WHERE schemaname = 'shared'\n387\t       ORDER BY tablename`,\n388\t    );\n389\t\n390\t    const found = new Set(result.rows.map((r) => r.tablename));\n391\t\n392\t    const missing = [...SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES].filter((t) => !found.has(t));\n393\t    const extra = [...found].filter((t) => !SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES.has(t));\n394\t\n395\t    if (missing.length > 0 || extra.length > 0) {\n396\t      const parts: string[] = [];\n397\t      if (missing.length > 0) {\n398\t        parts.push(`missing: ${missing.join(', ')}`);\n399\t      }\n400\t      if (extra.length > 0) {\n401\t        parts.push(`unexpected: ${extra.join(', ')}`);\n402\t      }\n403\t      throw new Error(\n404\t        `shared schema layout drift — ${parts.join('; ')}. ` +\n405\t          `The shared schema is the strict allow-list of ${SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES.size} tables. ` +\n406\t          `A new cross-service table requires an ADR drafted under docs/adr/ ` +\n407\t          `(template at docs/adr/011-schema-ownership-model.md) plus ` +\n408\t          `architectural-arbiter approval — see W5 add-shared-table skill gate ` +\n409\t          `(BLOCKER-15). Update SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES only after the ADR lands.`,\n410\t      );\n411\t    }\n412\t  });\n413\t\n414\t  it.each(MOVED_TABLES)(\n415\t    'table %s lives in %s schema (not public)',\n416\t    async (tableName, expectedSchema) => {\n417\t      const result = await db.query<{ schemaname: string }>(\n418\t        `SELECT schemaname FROM pg_tables WHERE tablename = $1`,\n419\t        [tableName],\n420\t      );\n421\t\n422\t      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n423\t        throw new Error(\n424\t          `Table \"${tableName}\" not found in any schema. The P6-P9 ` +\n425\t            `migration that moved it from public to ${expectedSchema} may ` +\n426\t            `have failed to apply, or the table was dropped without ` +\n427\t            `updating MOVED_TABLES.`,\n428\t        );\n429\t      }\n430\t\n431\t      const locations = result.rows.map((r) => r.schemaname);\n432\t      if (locations.includes('public')) {\n433\t        throw new Error(\n434\t          `Table \"${tableName}\" still exists in public schema after the ` +\n435\t            `teardown should have moved it to ${expectedSchema}. The ` +\n436\t            `MovePublicTablesTo<owner> migration may have been rolled back ` +\n437\t            `or never applied to this DB.`,\n438\t        );\n439\t      }\n440\t      if (!locations.includes(expectedSchema)) {\n441\t        throw new Error(\n442\t          `Table \"${tableName}\" expected in schema \"${expectedSchema}\" but ` +\n443\t            `found only in: ${locations.join(', ')}. ` +\n444\t            `Update MOVED_TABLES if the canonical home changed.`,\n445\t        );\n446\t      }\n447\t    },\n448\t  );\n449\t\n450\t  it.each(MESSAGING_TABLES)(\n451\t    'messaging-service table %s lives in %s schema (not public, not tenant_*)',\n452\t    async (tableName, expectedSchema) => {\n453\t      // Look only in non-tenant schemas — tenant_<uuid>.<table> clones are\n454\t      // a separate concern (managed by TenantSchemaSyncService) and may\n455\t      // legitimately exist alongside the source table.\n456\t      const result = await db.query<{ schemaname: string }>(\n457\t        `SELECT schemaname FROM pg_tables\n458\t         WHERE tablename = $1\n459\t           AND schemaname NOT LIKE 'tenant\\\\_%' ESCAPE '\\\\'\n460\t         ORDER BY schemaname`,\n461\t        [tableName],\n462\t      );\n463\t\n464\t      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n465\t        throw new Error(\n466\t          `Messaging table \"${tableName}\" not found in any non-tenant schema. ` +\n467\t            `The 1782300000000-AddTenantIdToMessageChildren or the original ` +\n468\t            `1711800000000-CreateMessagingTables migration may have failed to ` +\n469\t            `apply, or the table was dropped without updating MESSAGING_TABLES.`,\n470\t        );\n471\t      }\n472\t\n473\t      const locations = result.rows.map((r) => r.schemaname);\n474\t      if (locations.includes('public')) {\n475\t        throw new Error(\n476\t          `Messaging table \"${tableName}\" leaked into public schema. ` +\n477\t            `Verify the @Entity decorator declares { schema: 'messaging' } — ` +\n478\t            `ADR-011 mandates explicit schema decoration on every entity.`,\n479\t        );\n480\t      }\n481\t      if (!locations.includes(expectedSchema)) {\n482\t        throw new Error(\n483\t          `Messaging table \"${tableName}\" expected in \"${expectedSchema}\" but ` +\n484\t            `found only in: ${locations.join(', ')}.`,\n485\t        );\n486\t      }\n487\t    },\n488\t  );\n489\t\n490\t  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n491\t  // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — extended schema invariants (B.1, B.2, B.4, B.5)\n492\t  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n493\t\n494\t  // B.1 — No-default-public assertion.\n495\t  //\n496\t  // Every entity table arg in TypeORM's metadata storage MUST either\n497\t  // declare `schema:` explicitly OR belong to a service in\n498\t  // TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES. The latter exception exists because the\n499\t  // farm-pattern (codified in ADR-011 + the 2026-04-14 doc revision)\n500\t  // routes tenant-aware entities through SchemaManagerService at\n501\t  // provision time; the @Entity decorator stays schema-agnostic so a\n502\t  // single class can be cloned into both the source schema and every\n503\t  // tenant_<uuid> schema.\n504\t  //\n505\t  // Any other entity that omits `schema:` lands in `public` by default\n506\t  // — exactly the regression class the 2026-04-14 teardown plan was\n507\t  // built to prevent.\n508\t  it('B.1 — every entity declares schema: or belongs to a tenant-scoped service', () => {\n509\t    const tables = loadAllEntityTableArgs();\n510\t    const offenders: string[] = [];\n511\t    for (const t of tables) {\n512\t      // Skip class-table-inheritance child entities (no name override).\n513\t      // Those legitimately inherit the parent's schema.\n514\t      if (!t.tableName) continue;\n515\t      if (t.schema) continue; // explicit schema — pass.\n516\t      if (TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES.has(t.serviceName)) continue; // farm-pattern.\n517\t      offenders.push(\n518\t        `${t.serviceName}::${t.targetName} (table=${t.tableName}) at ${t.filePath} ` +\n519\t          `omits schema: AND service is not tenant-scoped`,\n520\t      );\n521\t    }\n522\t    if (offenders.length > 0) {\n523\t      throw new Error(\n524\t        `B.1 default-public regression — ${offenders.length} entity decorator(s) ` +\n525\t          `omit \\`schema:\\` from a non-tenant-scoped service. Add the explicit ` +\n526\t          `\\`schema: '<owner>'\\` option, or move the service into the tenant-scoped ` +\n527\t          `allow-list if it actually has tenant-clone semantics.\\n  ` +\n528\t          offenders.join('\\n  '),\n529\t      );\n530\t    }\n531\t  });\n532\t\n533\t  // B.2 — Declared-vs-physical schema parity.\n534\t  //\n535\t  // For every entity that DOES declare a schema, the table named in\n536\t  // information_schema.tables MUST live in that schema. Catches\n537\t  // entities that pin the wrong schema (e.g. typo \"shared\" → \"share\")\n538\t  // — TypeORM accepts the typo silently and routes the table to\n539\t  // public if the schema does not yet exist.\n540\t  it(\"B.2 — declared schema matches the table's physical schema\", async () => {\n541\t    const tables = loadAllEntityTableArgs();\n542\t    const drifts: string[] = [];\n543\t    for (const t of tables) {\n544\t      if (!t.schema || !t.tableName) continue; // covered by B.1.\n545\t      // Skip @ViewEntity-style metadata — TypeORM tags views with\n546\t      // `type: 'view'` but the discovery surface here doesn't carry\n547\t      // the type field, so we accept that physical absence is not\n548\t      // a B.2 drift signal (covered separately by ViewInvariants).\n549\t      const result = await db.query<{ schemaname: string }>(\n550\t        `SELECT schemaname FROM pg_tables\n551\t         WHERE tablename = $1\n552\t           AND schemaname NOT LIKE 'tenant\\\\_%' ESCAPE '\\\\'\n553\t           AND schemaname NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')\n554\t         ORDER BY (schemaname = $2) DESC, schemaname`,\n555\t        [t.tableName, t.schema],\n556\t      );\n557\t      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n558\t        // Table absent in non-tenant schemas — could be a tenant-only\n559\t        // table cloned into tenant_<uuid> schemas at provision. Skip\n560\t        // the parity check; B.5 covers tenant-clone surface.\n561\t        continue;\n562\t      }\n563\t      const locations = result.rows.map((r) => r.schemaname);\n564\t      if (!locations.includes(t.schema)) {\n565\t        drifts.push(\n566\t          `${t.serviceName}::${t.targetName} declares schema=\"${t.schema}\" but ` +\n567\t            `table \"${t.tableName}\" lives in ${locations.join(', ')}`,\n568\t        );\n569\t      }\n570\t    }\n571\t    if (drifts.length > 0) {\n572\t      throw new Error(\n573\t        `B.2 declared-vs-physical schema drift (${drifts.length}):\\n  ` + drifts.join('\\n  '),\n574\t      );\n575\t    }\n576\t  }, 30_000);\n577\t\n578\t  // B.4 — Per-service schema ownership at the role level.\n579\t  //\n580\t  // The security property is the SEPARATION, not merely that some role owns the\n581\t  // schema: DDL authority lives on a NOLOGIN `<svc>_schema_owner` role, while\n582\t  // the login role the service runs as (`<svc>_service`) holds DML only. A\n583\t  // compromised runtime credential therefore cannot drop or re-shape a table.\n584\t  // Bootstrap stage 008 (\"Least-Privilege Runtime Boundary\") establishes it:\n585\t  // `REASSIGN OWNED BY <runtime> TO <owner>` then `ALTER SCHEMA … OWNER TO\n586\t  // <owner>`.\n587\t  //\n588\t  // This assertion previously expected the schema to be owned by the RUNTIME\n589\t  // role and pointed at `00-init-schemas.sh` lines 89-128 — a file archived on\n590\t  // 2026-05-18, describing the pre-stage-008 model. It was never noticed because\n591\t  // the spec named `db-migration-check.yml` as its home but appeared only in\n592\t  // that workflow's `paths:` filters and was never executed by a step\n593\t  // (FARM-MEDIUM-303). Its remediation advice had become actively dangerous:\n594\t  // running the suggested `ALTER SCHEMA \"farm\" OWNER TO farm_service` would hand\n595\t  // DDL power back to the runtime login role and collapse the very boundary the\n596\t  // assertion claims to protect.\n597\t  //\n598\t  // The (schema, owner, runtime) triples are READ FROM stage 008 rather than\n599\t  // re-typed here. A hardcoded copy is what rotted the first time; parsing the\n600\t  // bootstrap means a schema added there is covered automatically and a role\n601\t  // renamed there cannot disagree with this gate.\n602\t  it.each(LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS.map((s) => [s.schema_name, s.owner_role, s.runtime_role]))(\n603\t    'B.4 — schema \"%s\" is owned by \"%s\", not by the runtime role \"%s\"',\n604\t    async (schemaName, ownerRole, runtimeRole) => {\n605\t      const result = await db.query<{ owner: string | null }>(\n606\t        `SELECT pg_get_userbyid(nspowner) AS owner\n607\t       FROM pg_namespace\n608\t       WHERE nspname = $1`,\n609\t        [schemaName],\n610\t      );\n611\t      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n612\t        throw new Error(\n613\t          `B.4 schema \"${schemaName}\" does not exist in pg_namespace. ` +\n614\t            `Bootstrap stage 003 creates it; verify aqua-db-migrate ran against this database.`,\n615\t        );\n616\t      }\n617\t      const owner = result.rows[0]?.owner;\n618\t      if (owner === runtimeRole) {\n619\t        throw new Error(\n620\t          `B.4 schema \"${schemaName}\" is owned by its RUNTIME role \"${runtimeRole}\". ` +\n621\t            `That gives the service's login credential DDL authority over its own schema, ` +\n622\t            `which is the least-privilege boundary bootstrap stage 008 exists to close. ` +\n623\t            `Re-run stage 008, or \\`ALTER SCHEMA \"${schemaName}\" OWNER TO ${ownerRole}\\`.`,\n624\t        );\n625\t      }\n626\t      if (owner !== ownerRole) {\n627\t        throw new Error(\n628\t          `B.4 schema \"${schemaName}\" is owned by \"${owner}\", expected \"${ownerRole}\". ` +\n629\t            `Bootstrap stage 008 assigns ownership to the NOLOGIN owner role; a schema ` +\n630\t            `created manually by a superuser keeps \\`postgres\\` as owner and never gets ` +\n631\t            `the boundary. Fix via \\`ALTER SCHEMA \"${schemaName}\" OWNER TO ${ownerRole}\\`.`,\n632\t        );\n633\t      }\n634\t    },\n635\t  );\n636\t\n637\t  // The owner roles must not be able to log in: the boundary is only real while\n638\t  // DDL authority is reachable through role membership (db_migrate) rather than\n639\t  // through a credential someone can present.\n640\t  it('B.4b — schema-owner roles are NOLOGIN', async () => {\n641\t    const owners = LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS.map((s) => s.owner_role);\n642\t    const result = await db.query<{ rolname: string }>(\n643\t      `SELECT rolname FROM pg_catalog.pg_roles\n644\t        WHERE rolname = ANY($1::text[]) AND rolcanlogin`,\n645\t      [owners],\n646\t    );\n647\t    const offenders = result.rows.map(\n648\t      (row) =>\n649\t        `${row.rolname} can log in — a schema-owner role is DDL authority and must be ` +\n650\t        `reachable only through GRANT <owner> TO db_migrate, never as a credential`,\n651\t    );\n652\t    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);\n653\t  });\n654\t\n655\t  // B.5a — TENANT_SCOPED schemas: each MUST have at least one\n656\t  // tenant_<uuid> clone of its source tables in the test DB.\n657\t  // Runs only when the test DB has at least one provisioned tenant\n658\t  // (the farm-service migration baseline + e2e fixtures provision a\n659\t  // smoke tenant). If no tenant exists, the assertion is vacuously\n660\t  // satisfied — the bootstrap path doesn't provision tenants by\n661\t  // itself.\n662\t  it.each(TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS)(\n663\t    'B.5a — tenant-scoped schema \"%s\" has at least one tenant clone (when tenants exist)',\n664\t    async (sourceSchema) => {\n665\t      // Inventory of tenant clone schemas in the DB.\n666\t      const tenantSchemas = await db.query<{ schema_name: string }>(\n667\t        `SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata\n668\t         WHERE schema_name ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'`,\n669\t      );\n670\t      if (tenantSchemas.rows.length === 0) {\n671\t        // No tenants provisioned — assertion vacuously true. Part C\n672\t        // spec actively provisions a tenant and runs the same check\n673\t        // with stronger preconditions.\n674\t        return;\n675\t      }\n676\t      // Sample one fan-out table from the SchemaManagerService SSoT.\n677\t      // Source schemas may also contain source-only infrastructure\n678\t      // tables (migrations, outbox, audit ledgers) that must NOT be\n679\t      // tenant-cloned.\n680\t      const sourceTables = TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA.get(sourceSchema) ?? [];\n681\t      const sampleTable = [...sourceTables].sort()[0];\n682\t      if (!sampleTable) {\n683\t        // No fan-out tables declared — nothing to clone, nothing to\n684\t        // assert. MODULE_SCHEMAS static invariants cover declaration\n685\t        // completeness.\n686\t        return;\n687\t      }\n688\t      const cloneCheck = await db.query<{ count: string }>(\n689\t        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM information_schema.tables\n690\t         WHERE table_schema ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n691\t           AND table_name = $1`,\n692\t        [sampleTable],\n693\t      );\n694\t      const cloneCount = parseInt(cloneCheck.rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10);\n695\t      if (cloneCount === 0) {\n696\t        throw new Error(\n697\t          `B.5a tenant-scoped schema \"${sourceSchema}\" has source table ` +\n698\t            `\"${sampleTable}\" but ZERO tenant_<uuid> schemas carry a clone of it. ` +\n699\t            `TenantSchemaSyncService is not provisioning the table on tenant ` +\n700\t            `creation, or every existing tenant predates the table's introduction ` +\n701\t            `(in which case run the deploy-time tenant fan-out).`,\n702\t        );\n703\t      }\n704\t    },\n705\t  );\n706\t\n707\t  // B.5b — PLATFORM_LEVEL schemas: each MUST have ZERO tenant_<uuid>\n708\t  // clones of its tables. A tenant clone of e.g. `auth.users` is a\n709\t  // tenant-isolation hole — auth is the cross-tenant trust anchor.\n710\t  it.each(PLATFORM_LEVEL_SCHEMAS)(\n711\t    'B.5b — platform-level schema \"%s\" has ZERO tenant_<uuid> clones of its tables',\n712\t    async (platformSchema) => {\n713\t      const sourceTables = await db.query<{ table_name: string }>(\n714\t        `SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables\n715\t         WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'`,\n716\t        [platformSchema],\n717\t      );\n718\t      const sourceTableNames = sourceTables.rows\n719\t        .map((r) => r.table_name)\n720\t        .filter((tableName) => !REGISTERED_TENANT_TABLE_NAMES.has(tableName));\n721\t      if (sourceTableNames.length === 0) return;\n722\t\n723\t      const violations = await db.query<{ table_schema: string; table_name: string }>(\n724\t        `SELECT table_schema, table_name FROM information_schema.tables\n725\t         WHERE table_schema ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n726\t           AND table_name = ANY($1::text[])`,\n727\t        [sourceTableNames],\n728\t      );\n729\t      if (violations.rows.length > 0) {\n730\t        const sample = violations.rows\n731\t          .slice(0, 5)\n732\t          .map((r) => `${r.table_schema}.${r.table_name}`)\n733\t          .join(', ');\n734\t        throw new Error(\n735\t          `B.5b platform-level schema \"${platformSchema}\" has ${violations.rows.length} ` +\n736\t            `tenant_<uuid>.<table> clone(s) of its tables — first 5: ${sample}. ` +\n737\t            `Platform schemas are global by definition; per-tenant clones violate ` +\n738\t            `the trust-boundary model. Investigate which migration or provisioning ` +\n739\t            `path created them and remove via DROP TABLE on the offending tenant ` +\n740\t            `clones.`,\n741\t        );\n742\t      }\n743\t    },\n744\t  );\n745\t});\n746\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8070c131-7617-49fa-b7e0-d0733efc07c0","uuid":"4270c208-f39d-4ea0-ba11-3c793981eb5b","timestamp":"2026-10-08T01:51:10.136Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-bacb8bed08ee/e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Schema Invariants\n * ============================================================================\n *\n * Asserts the architectural invariants established by the 2026-04-14\n * public-schema teardown plan (docs/plans/2026-04-14-...). These are\n * static facts about the database — no service interactions, no tenant\n * fixtures, just SQL against the live test DB.\n *\n * # Why this test exists\n *\n * Before the teardown, services silently leaked tables into `public`\n * because @Entity decorators omitted the `schema:` option. The drift\n * went undetected until services failed to bootstrap RLS and a\n * production multi-tenant isolation hole opened. A CI invariant on the\n * physical schema layout makes that class of regression impossible by\n * construction — any new entity that forgets `schema:` lands in public,\n * the test below fails, the build is rejected.\n *\n * # What it checks\n *\n *   1. `public` schema contains ONLY the migrations meta + PostgreSQL\n *      extension artifacts. Zero application tables.\n *\n *   2. The `shared` schema contains exactly the four genuinely\n *      cross-service tables (audit_logs, gdpr_data_requests,\n *      user_consents, access_logs).\n *\n *   3. Every table that was moved during P6-P9 lives in its expected\n *      owning schema (and NOT in public).\n *\n * # When this test fails\n *\n *   - A new entity was added without `schema:` → relocate the entity\n *     by adding the missing decorator option.\n *   - A migration moved a table to public for a transition phase →\n *     either complete the move forward in the same PR or update this\n *     test's allowed-list with a justification comment.\n *   - The shared schema acquired or lost a table → either codify the\n *     change in this allow-list (with PR review on the change) or\n *     revert the migration.\n */\n\nimport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'fs';\nimport { createRequire } from 'module';\nimport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n\nimport { PROTECTED_TABLES } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/constants';\nimport { MODULE_SCHEMAS } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/database';\nimport { getMetadataArgsStorage } from 'typeorm';\n\nimport { TestDatabase } from '../../helpers/db.helper';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..');\n\nconst requireEntity = createRequire(__filename);\n\n/**\n * Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — TENANT_SCOPED vs PLATFORM_LEVEL audit lists.\n *\n * Hardcodes the operator's clarification (Dalga-5 brief): exactly\n * which schemas spawn `tenant_<uuid>` clones vs which stay platform-\n * global. The two sets together cover every owned schema in the SaaS\n * topology; their union, plus `shared` + `public`, is the full\n * inventory.\n *\n * - TENANT_SCOPED schemas MUST have at least one tenant_<uuid> clone\n *   in the active test DB (assertion B.5a). The presence of clones\n *   is what makes \"schema-per-tenant\" visible at the storage layer.\n * - PLATFORM_LEVEL schemas MUST have ZERO tenant_<uuid>.<sametable>\n *   clones (assertion B.5b). A clone of `auth.users` would be a\n *   tenant-isolation hole — auth is the cross-tenant trust anchor\n *   per CLAUDE.md \"tenant row placement (D14)\".\n */\nconst TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n  'farm',\n  'sensor',\n  'hr',\n  'messaging',\n  'hydroponics',\n  'ai',\n  'alert',\n];\n\nconst PLATFORM_LEVEL_SCHEMAS: ReadonlyArray<string> = [\n  'auth',\n  'billing',\n  'admin',\n  'notification',\n  'event_store',\n  'observability',\n  'config',\n  'gateway',\n  'shared',\n  // Registry-completeness sweep (ORPHAN-HIGH-365): compliance (legal_holds,\n  // admin-api-owned) + platform (db-migrate bootstrap tables) — single\n  // cross-tenant schemas, never per-tenant cloned.\n  'compliance',\n  'platform',\n];\n\nconst TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyArray<string>> = new Map(\n  MODULE_SCHEMAS.map((moduleSchema) => [moduleSchema.sourceSchema, moduleSchema.tables]),\n);\n\n/**\n * Every table name a tenant schema is SUPPOSED to carry, from the registry.\n *\n * B.5b looks for platform-schema tables cloned into a tenant, and it can only\n * compare by NAME — `information_schema` has no notion of \"clone of\". Names are\n * not unique across schemas, so a per-tenant table whose name also exists in a\n * platform schema reads as a trust-boundary violation when it is nothing of the\n * kind: `messages` lives in `auth` and `admin` AND is messaging's per-tenant\n * table; `retention_policies` and `legal_holds` collide the same way.\n *\n * That never surfaced because B.5b had never run against a database with a real\n * tenant in it — B.5a was passing vacuously against zero tenants for the same\n * reason. The first genuinely provisioned tenant produced four false positives\n * and no true ones.\n *\n * Subtracting the registered per-tenant names keeps the assertion pointed at\n * what it means: a tenant table that is a clone of something only a platform\n * schema owns. A platform table that ALSO gets registered per-tenant would stop\n * being caught here — but that registration is itself the violation, and\n * `entity-schema-declaration` and B.1/B.2 are the gates that own it.\n */\nconst REGISTERED_TENANT_TABLE_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(\n  MODULE_SCHEMAS.flatMap((moduleSchema) => [\n    ...moduleSchema.tables,\n    ...(moduleSchema.referenceDataTables ?? []),\n  ]),\n);\n\n// The TENANT_SCOPED set is also the allow-list for entities legitimately\n// declaring `schema: undefined` (farm-pattern: SchemaManagerService\n// routes them at provision time, so the @Entity decorator stays\n// schema-agnostic). Any entity outside this allow-list that omits\n// `schema:` is a regression — see assertion B.1.\nconst TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n  'farm-service',\n  'sensor-service',\n  'hr-service',\n  'messaging-service',\n  'hydroponics-service',\n  'ai-service',\n  'alert-engine',\n]);\n\n/**\n * Discover entity files under `apps/<service>/src` for static @Entity\n * audit (B.1, B.2). Mirrors the bootstrap-from-scratch helper but lives\n * separately so this spec has no cross-spec import dependency.\n */\nfunction discoverEntityFiles(): Array<{ service: string; file: string }> {\n  const out: Array<{ service: string; file: string }> = [];\n  const appsRoot = join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n  if (!existsSync(appsRoot)) return out;\n  for (const svc of readdirSync(appsRoot)) {\n    const svcRoot = join(appsRoot, svc, 'src');\n    if (!existsSync(svcRoot)) continue;\n    const stack: string[] = [svcRoot];\n    while (stack.length > 0) {\n      const cur = stack.pop();\n      if (!cur) continue;\n      let entries: string[];\n      try {\n        entries = readdirSync(cur);\n      } catch {\n        continue;\n      }\n      for (const name of entries) {\n        if (name === 'migrations' || name === '__tests__' || name === 'node_modules') continue;\n        const full = join(cur, name);\n        let st;\n        try {\n          st = statSync(full);\n        } catch {\n          continue;\n        }\n        if (st.isDirectory()) stack.push(full);\n        else if (st.isFile() && /\\.entity\\.ts$/.test(name)) {\n          out.push({ service: svc, file: full });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/**\n * Load every @Entity registered with TypeORM by requiring each entity\n * file. Duplicates protection: getMetadataArgsStorage() is a global\n * singleton; we read its `tables` array AFTER all requires complete.\n */\nfunction loadAllEntityTableArgs(): Array<{\n  serviceName: string;\n  filePath: string;\n  schema: string | undefined;\n  tableName: string | undefined;\n  targetName: string;\n}> {\n  const files = discoverEntityFiles();\n  // Map target class -> the service+file that registered it. We\n  // populate this BEFORE require() to attribute drift back to the\n  // owning service when getMetadataArgsStorage() reports a target.\n  const ownership = new Map<object, { service: string; file: string }>();\n  for (const { service, file } of files) {\n    let mod: Record<string, unknown>;\n    try {\n      mod = requireEntity(file) as Record<string, unknown>;\n    } catch {\n      continue;\n    }\n    for (const v of Object.values(mod)) {\n      if (typeof v === 'function' && /^[A-Z]/.test(v.name) && !ownership.has(v)) {\n        ownership.set(v, { service, file });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const storage = getMetadataArgsStorage();\n  return storage.tables.map((t) => {\n    const target = typeof t.target === 'function' ? t.target : null;\n    const own = target ? ownership.get(target) : undefined;\n    return {\n      serviceName: own?.service ?? 'unknown',\n      filePath: own?.file ?? '<unknown>',\n      schema: t.schema,\n      tableName: typeof t.name === 'string' ? t.name : undefined,\n      targetName: target?.name ?? 'unknown-target',\n    };\n  });\n}\n\nconst ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES = new Set<string>([\n  // TypeORM migration ledger — system table, not application data.\n  'migrations',\n]);\n\n/** One row of bootstrap stage 008's least-privilege role table. */\ninterface LeastPrivilegeSpec {\n  readonly schema_name: string;\n  readonly owner_role: string;\n  readonly runtime_role: string;\n  readonly provisioner_role: string | null;\n}\n\nconst LEAST_PRIVILEGE_STAGE = resolve(\n  REPO_ROOT,\n  'apps/db-migrate/src/sql/platform-bootstrap/008-least-privilege-hardening.sql',\n);\n\n/**\n * The (schema, owner role, runtime role) triples, READ FROM the bootstrap stage\n * that establishes them.\n *\n * B.4 used to carry its own hardcoded list. It drifted — it still expected the\n * pre-stage-008 model where the runtime role owned the schema — and nothing\n * caught the drift because the spec was never executed (FARM-MEDIUM-303). A\n * second copy of a security-relevant mapping is exactly the thing that goes\n * stale silently, so this one is derived: the stage embeds its table as a JSON\n * array literal fed to `jsonb_to_recordset`, which is machine-readable as-is.\n *\n * Parse failure THROWS at module load rather than yielding an empty list — an\n * empty list would turn every B.4 case into a no-op and the gate would report\n * green while asserting nothing.\n */\nfunction readLeastPrivilegeSpecs(): readonly LeastPrivilegeSpec[] {\n  const sql = readFileSync(LEAST_PRIVILEGE_STAGE, 'utf8');\n  const match = sql.match(/jsonb_to_recordset\\(\\s*'(\\[[\\s\\S]*?\\])'::jsonb/);\n  if (!match?.[1]) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `B.4 could not read the least-privilege role table from ${LEAST_PRIVILEGE_STAGE}. ` +\n        \"The stage still exists but its `jsonb_to_recordset('[...]'::jsonb)` literal no \" +\n        'longer matches — update this parser rather than re-hardcoding the roles here.',\n    );\n  }\n  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(match[1]);\n  if (!Array.isArray(parsed) || parsed.length === 0) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `B.4 least-privilege role table parsed to an empty list — refusing to run vacuously.`,\n    );\n  }\n  return parsed as LeastPrivilegeSpec[];\n}\n\nconst LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS = readLeastPrivilegeSpecs();\n\n// ORPHAN-179: derive the shared-schema canonical table set from the\n// PROTECTED_TABLES SSoT (libs/backend-common/.../protected-tables.ts) instead of\n// hand-copying it (this was the 4th unguarded copy). PROTECTED_TABLES already\n// carries the `shared.*` entries — audit_logs (7y), gdpr_data_requests,\n// user_consents, access_logs (request-level, 90d; survives\n// tenant deletion for forensics). One list, no drift.\n// user_permissions retired 2026-07-12 (ADR-042, ORPHAN-HIGH-378).\nconst SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES = new Set<string>(\n  PROTECTED_TABLES.filter((t) => t.startsWith('shared.')).map((t) => t.slice('shared.'.length)),\n);\n\n/**\n * Tables moved out of `public` during P6-P9 of the teardown. Each entry\n * is `[tableName, expectedSchema]` and is asserted both directions:\n * the table EXISTS in expectedSchema and DOES NOT exist in public.\n */\nconst MOVED_TABLES: Array<[string, string]> = [\n  // P6 — low-risk singletons\n  ['channel_detection_log', 'sensor'],\n  ['device_tokens', 'notification'],\n  ['weather_settings', 'farm'],\n  ['sentinel_hub_settings', 'farm'],\n  // P7 — moderate\n  ['sensor_type_definitions', 'sensor'],\n  ['feeder_calibrations', 'farm'],\n  ['marine_observations', 'farm'],\n  ['weather_observations', 'farm'],\n  ['notification_logs', 'notification'],\n  // P8 — high-complexity\n  ['tenant_roles', 'auth'],\n  ['employees', 'hr'],\n];\n\n/**\n * Messaging service tables — established in their messaging-schema home\n * by the 2026-04-14 messaging-isolation plan (P7 entity decoration).\n * Asserts each of the 17 messaging entities lives in `messaging` and\n * NOT in `public`. Same assertion semantics as MOVED_TABLES.\n */\nconst MESSAGING_TABLES: Array<[string, string]> = [\n  ['channels', 'messaging'],\n  ['channel_members', 'messaging'],\n  ['messages', 'messaging'],\n  ['message_attachments', 'messaging'],\n  ['message_receipts', 'messaging'],\n  ['message_reactions', 'messaging'],\n  ['pinned_messages', 'messaging'],\n  ['messaging_outbox', 'messaging'],\n  ['retention_policies', 'messaging'],\n  ['legal_holds', 'messaging'],\n  ['compliance_audit_log', 'messaging'],\n  ['message_analysis', 'messaging'],\n  ['message_entity_references', 'messaging'],\n  ['knowledge_entries', 'messaging'],\n  ['embeddings_metadata', 'messaging'],\n  ['user_ai_consents', 'messaging'],\n];\n\ndescribe('Schema Invariants (2026-04-14 public-schema teardown)', () => {\n  const db = new TestDatabase();\n\n  afterAll(async () => {\n    await db.close();\n  });\n\n  it('public schema contains no application tables', async () => {\n    const result = await db.query<{ tablename: string }>(\n      `SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables\n       WHERE schemaname = 'public'\n       ORDER BY tablename`,\n    );\n\n    const offenders = result.rows\n      .map((r) => r.tablename)\n      .filter((name) => !ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES.has(name));\n\n    if (offenders.length > 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `public schema must contain only ${[...ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES].join(', ')}, ` +\n          `but found extra tables: ${offenders.join(', ')}. ` +\n          `Either move them to their owning service schema (add ` +\n          `\\`schema: '<owner>'\\` to the @Entity decorator + write a ` +\n          `SET SCHEMA migration), OR codify them in ALLOWED_PUBLIC_TABLES ` +\n          `with a PR review explaining why public ownership is correct.`,\n      );\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('shared schema strict allow-list (B.3) — exactly the 4 canonical cross-service tables', async () => {\n    // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — B.3 strict allow-list, reduced to 4 entries by\n    // ADR-042 (user_permissions retired, ORPHAN-HIGH-378).\n    // The 4 canonical tables are: audit_logs, gdpr_data_requests,\n    // user_consents, access_logs. A 5th requires\n    // an ADR per ADR-011 + architectural-arbiter approval (W5\n    // `add-shared-table` skill gate — BLOCKER-15).\n    const result = await db.query<{ tablename: string }>(\n      `SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables\n       WHERE schemaname = 'shared'\n       ORDER BY tablename`,\n    );\n\n    const found = new Set(result.rows.map((r) => r.tablename));\n\n    const missing = [...SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES].filter((t) => !found.has(t));\n    const extra = [...found].filter((t) => !SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES.has(t));\n\n    if (missing.length > 0 || extra.length > 0) {\n      const parts: string[] = [];\n      if (missing.length > 0) {\n        parts.push(`missing: ${missing.join(', ')}`);\n      }\n      if (extra.length > 0) {\n        parts.push(`unexpected: ${extra.join(', ')}`);\n      }\n      throw new Error(\n        `shared schema layout drift — ${parts.join('; ')}. ` +\n          `The shared schema is the strict allow-list of ${SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES.size} tables. ` +\n          `A new cross-service table requires an ADR drafted under docs/adr/ ` +\n          `(template at docs/adr/011-schema-ownership-model.md) plus ` +\n          `architectural-arbiter approval — see W5 add-shared-table skill gate ` +\n          `(BLOCKER-15). Update SHARED_SCHEMA_TABLES only after the ADR lands.`,\n      );\n    }\n  });\n\n  it.each(MOVED_TABLES)(\n    'table %s lives in %s schema (not public)',\n    async (tableName, expectedSchema) => {\n      const result = await db.query<{ schemaname: string }>(\n        `SELECT schemaname FROM pg_tables WHERE tablename = $1`,\n        [tableName],\n      );\n\n      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Table \"${tableName}\" not found in any schema. The P6-P9 ` +\n            `migration that moved it from public to ${expectedSchema} may ` +\n            `have failed to apply, or the table was dropped without ` +\n            `updating MOVED_TABLES.`,\n        );\n      }\n\n      const locations = result.rows.map((r) => r.schemaname);\n      if (locations.includes('public')) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Table \"${tableName}\" still exists in public schema after the ` +\n            `teardown should have moved it to ${expectedSchema}. The ` +\n            `MovePublicTablesTo<owner> migration may have been rolled back ` +\n            `or never applied to this DB.`,\n        );\n      }\n      if (!locations.includes(expectedSchema)) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Table \"${tableName}\" expected in schema \"${expectedSchema}\" but ` +\n            `found only in: ${locations.join(', ')}. ` +\n            `Update MOVED_TABLES if the canonical home changed.`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  it.each(MESSAGING_TABLES)(\n    'messaging-service table %s lives in %s schema (not public, not tenant_*)',\n    async (tableName, expectedSchema) => {\n      // Look only in non-tenant schemas — tenant_<uuid>.<table> clones are\n      // a separate concern (managed by TenantSchemaSyncService) and may\n      // legitimately exist alongside the source table.\n      const result = await db.query<{ schemaname: string }>(\n        `SELECT schemaname FROM pg_tables\n         WHERE tablename = $1\n           AND schemaname NOT LIKE 'tenant\\\\_%' ESCAPE '\\\\'\n         ORDER BY schemaname`,\n        [tableName],\n      );\n\n      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Messaging table \"${tableName}\" not found in any non-tenant schema. ` +\n            `The 1782300000000-AddTenantIdToMessageChildren or the original ` +\n            `1711800000000-CreateMessagingTables migration may have failed to ` +\n            `apply, or the table was dropped without updating MESSAGING_TABLES.`,\n        );\n      }\n\n      const locations = result.rows.map((r) => r.schemaname);\n      if (locations.includes('public')) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Messaging table \"${tableName}\" leaked into public schema. ` +\n            `Verify the @Entity decorator declares { schema: 'messaging' } — ` +\n            `ADR-011 mandates explicit schema decoration on every entity.`,\n        );\n      }\n      if (!locations.includes(expectedSchema)) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Messaging table \"${tableName}\" expected in \"${expectedSchema}\" but ` +\n            `found only in: ${locations.join(', ')}.`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — extended schema invariants (B.1, B.2, B.4, B.5)\n  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  // B.1 — No-default-public assertion.\n  //\n  // Every entity table arg in TypeORM's metadata storage MUST either\n  // declare `schema:` explicitly OR belong to a service in\n  // TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES. The latter exception exists because the\n  // farm-pattern (codified in ADR-011 + the 2026-04-14 doc revision)\n  // routes tenant-aware entities through SchemaManagerService at\n  // provision time; the @Entity decorator stays schema-agnostic so a\n  // single class can be cloned into both the source schema and every\n  // tenant_<uuid> schema.\n  //\n  // Any other entity that omits `schema:` lands in `public` by default\n  // — exactly the regression class the 2026-04-14 teardown plan was\n  // built to prevent.\n  it('B.1 — every entity declares schema: or belongs to a tenant-scoped service', () => {\n    const tables = loadAllEntityTableArgs();\n    const offenders: string[] = [];\n    for (const t of tables) {\n      // Skip class-table-inheritance child entities (no name override).\n      // Those legitimately inherit the parent's schema.\n      if (!t.tableName) continue;\n      if (t.schema) continue; // explicit schema — pass.\n      if (TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES.has(t.serviceName)) continue; // farm-pattern.\n      offenders.push(\n        `${t.serviceName}::${t.targetName} (table=${t.tableName}) at ${t.filePath} ` +\n          `omits schema: AND service is not tenant-scoped`,\n      );\n    }\n    if (offenders.length > 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `B.1 default-public regression — ${offenders.length} entity decorator(s) ` +\n          `omit \\`schema:\\` from a non-tenant-scoped service. Add the explicit ` +\n          `\\`schema: '<owner>'\\` option, or move the service into the tenant-scoped ` +\n          `allow-list if it actually has tenant-clone semantics.\\n  ` +\n          offenders.join('\\n  '),\n      );\n    }\n  });\n\n  // B.2 — Declared-vs-physical schema parity.\n  //\n  // For every entity that DOES declare a schema, the table named in\n  // information_schema.tables MUST live in that schema. Catches\n  // entities that pin the wrong schema (e.g. typo \"shared\" → \"share\")\n  // — TypeORM accepts the typo silently and routes the table to\n  // public if the schema does not yet exist.\n  it(\"B.2 — declared schema matches the table's physical schema\", async () => {\n    const tables = loadAllEntityTableArgs();\n    const drifts: string[] = [];\n    for (const t of tables) {\n      if (!t.schema || !t.tableName) continue; // covered by B.1.\n      // Skip @ViewEntity-style metadata — TypeORM tags views with\n      // `type: 'view'` but the discovery surface here doesn't carry\n      // the type field, so we accept that physical absence is not\n      // a B.2 drift signal (covered separately by ViewInvariants).\n      const result = await db.query<{ schemaname: string }>(\n        `SELECT schemaname FROM pg_tables\n         WHERE tablename = $1\n           AND schemaname NOT LIKE 'tenant\\\\_%' ESCAPE '\\\\'\n           AND schemaname NOT IN ('pg_catalog', 'information_schema')\n         ORDER BY (schemaname = $2) DESC, schemaname`,\n        [t.tableName, t.schema],\n      );\n      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n        // Table absent in non-tenant schemas — could be a tenant-only\n        // table cloned into tenant_<uuid> schemas at provision. Skip\n        // the parity check; B.5 covers tenant-clone surface.\n        continue;\n      }\n      const locations = result.rows.map((r) => r.schemaname);\n      if (!locations.includes(t.schema)) {\n        drifts.push(\n          `${t.serviceName}::${t.targetName} declares schema=\"${t.schema}\" but ` +\n            `table \"${t.tableName}\" lives in ${locations.join(', ')}`,\n        );\n      }\n    }\n    if (drifts.length > 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `B.2 declared-vs-physical schema drift (${drifts.length}):\\n  ` + drifts.join('\\n  '),\n      );\n    }\n  }, 30_000);\n\n  // B.4 — Per-service schema ownership at the role level.\n  //\n  // The security property is the SEPARATION, not merely that some role owns the\n  // schema: DDL authority lives on a NOLOGIN `<svc>_schema_owner` role, while\n  // the login role the service runs as (`<svc>_service`) holds DML only. A\n  // compromised runtime credential therefore cannot drop or re-shape a table.\n  // Bootstrap stage 008 (\"Least-Privilege Runtime Boundary\") establishes it:\n  // `REASSIGN OWNED BY <runtime> TO <owner>` then `ALTER SCHEMA … OWNER TO\n  // <owner>`.\n  //\n  // This assertion previously expected the schema to be owned by the RUNTIME\n  // role and pointed at `00-init-schemas.sh` lines 89-128 — a file archived on\n  // 2026-05-18, describing the pre-stage-008 model. It was never noticed because\n  // the spec named `db-migration-check.yml` as its home but appeared only in\n  // that workflow's `paths:` filters and was never executed by a step\n  // (FARM-MEDIUM-303). Its remediation advice had become actively dangerous:\n  // running the suggested `ALTER SCHEMA \"farm\" OWNER TO farm_service` would hand\n  // DDL power back to the runtime login role and collapse the very boundary the\n  // assertion claims to protect.\n  //\n  // The (schema, owner, runtime) triples are READ FROM stage 008 rather than\n  // re-typed here. A hardcoded copy is what rotted the first time; parsing the\n  // bootstrap means a schema added there is covered automatically and a role\n  // renamed there cannot disagree with this gate.\n  it.each(LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS.map((s) => [s.schema_name, s.owner_role, s.runtime_role]))(\n    'B.4 — schema \"%s\" is owned by \"%s\", not by the runtime role \"%s\"',\n    async (schemaName, ownerRole, runtimeRole) => {\n      const result = await db.query<{ owner: string | null }>(\n        `SELECT pg_get_userbyid(nspowner) AS owner\n       FROM pg_namespace\n       WHERE nspname = $1`,\n        [schemaName],\n      );\n      if (result.rows.length === 0) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `B.4 schema \"${schemaName}\" does not exist in pg_namespace. ` +\n            `Bootstrap stage 003 creates it; verify aqua-db-migrate ran against this database.`,\n        );\n      }\n      const owner = result.rows[0]?.owner;\n      if (owner === runtimeRole) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `B.4 schema \"${schemaName}\" is owned by its RUNTIME role \"${runtimeRole}\". ` +\n            `That gives the service's login credential DDL authority over its own schema, ` +\n            `which is the least-privilege boundary bootstrap stage 008 exists to close. ` +\n            `Re-run stage 008, or \\`ALTER SCHEMA \"${schemaName}\" OWNER TO ${ownerRole}\\`.`,\n        );\n      }\n      if (owner !== ownerRole) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `B.4 schema \"${schemaName}\" is owned by \"${owner}\", expected \"${ownerRole}\". ` +\n            `Bootstrap stage 008 assigns ownership to the NOLOGIN owner role; a schema ` +\n            `created manually by a superuser keeps \\`postgres\\` as owner and never gets ` +\n            `the boundary. Fix via \\`ALTER SCHEMA \"${schemaName}\" OWNER TO ${ownerRole}\\`.`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  // The owner roles must not be able to log in: the boundary is only real while\n  // DDL authority is reachable through role membership (db_migrate) rather than\n  // through a credential someone can present.\n  it('B.4b — schema-owner roles are NOLOGIN', async () => {\n    const owners = LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS.map((s) => s.owner_role);\n    const result = await db.query<{ rolname: string }>(\n      `SELECT rolname FROM pg_catalog.pg_roles\n        WHERE rolname = ANY($1::text[]) AND rolcanlogin`,\n      [owners],\n    );\n    const offenders = result.rows.map(\n      (row) =>\n        `${row.rolname} can log in — a schema-owner role is DDL authority and must be ` +\n        `reachable only through GRANT <owner> TO db_migrate, never as a credential`,\n    );\n    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  // B.5a — TENANT_SCOPED schemas: each MUST have at least one\n  // tenant_<uuid> clone of its source tables in the test DB.\n  // Runs only when the test DB has at least one provisioned tenant\n  // (the farm-service migration baseline + e2e fixtures provision a\n  // smoke tenant). If no tenant exists, the assertion is vacuously\n  // satisfied — the bootstrap path doesn't provision tenants by\n  // itself.\n  it.each(TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS)(\n    'B.5a — tenant-scoped schema \"%s\" has at least one tenant clone (when tenants exist)',\n    async (sourceSchema) => {\n      // Inventory of tenant clone schemas in the DB.\n      const tenantSchemas = await db.query<{ schema_name: string }>(\n        `SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata\n         WHERE schema_name ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'`,\n      );\n      if (tenantSchemas.rows.length === 0) {\n        // No tenants provisioned — assertion vacuously true. Part C\n        // spec actively provisions a tenant and runs the same check\n        // with stronger preconditions.\n        return;\n      }\n      // Sample one fan-out table from the SchemaManagerService SSoT.\n      // Source schemas may also contain source-only infrastructure\n      // tables (migrations, outbox, audit ledgers) that must NOT be\n      // tenant-cloned.\n      const sourceTables = TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA.get(sourceSchema) ?? [];\n      const sampleTable = [...sourceTables].sort()[0];\n      if (!sampleTable) {\n        // No fan-out tables declared — nothing to clone, nothing to\n        // assert. MODULE_SCHEMAS static invariants cover declaration\n        // completeness.\n        return;\n      }\n      const cloneCheck = await db.query<{ count: string }>(\n        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM information_schema.tables\n         WHERE table_schema ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n           AND table_name = $1`,\n        [sampleTable],\n      );\n      const cloneCount = parseInt(cloneCheck.rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10);\n      if (cloneCount === 0) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `B.5a tenant-scoped schema \"${sourceSchema}\" has source table ` +\n            `\"${sampleTable}\" but ZERO tenant_<uuid> schemas carry a clone of it. ` +\n            `TenantSchemaSyncService is not provisioning the table on tenant ` +\n            `creation, or every existing tenant predates the table's introduction ` +\n            `(in which case run the deploy-time tenant fan-out).`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  // B.5b — PLATFORM_LEVEL schemas: each MUST have ZERO tenant_<uuid>\n  // clones of its tables. A tenant clone of e.g. `auth.users` is a\n  // tenant-isolation hole — auth is the cross-tenant trust anchor.\n  it.each(PLATFORM_LEVEL_SCHEMAS)(\n    'B.5b — platform-level schema \"%s\" has ZERO tenant_<uuid> clones of its tables',\n    async (platformSchema) => {\n      const sourceTables = await db.query<{ table_name: string }>(\n        `SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables\n         WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'`,\n        [platformSchema],\n      );\n      const sourceTableNames = sourceTables.rows\n        .map((r) => r.table_name)\n        .filter((tableName) => !REGISTERED_TENANT_TABLE_NAMES.has(tableName));\n      if (sourceTableNames.length === 0) return;\n\n      const violations = await db.query<{ table_schema: string; table_name: string }>(\n        `SELECT table_schema, table_name FROM information_schema.tables\n         WHERE table_schema ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n           AND table_name = ANY($1::text[])`,\n        [sourceTableNames],\n      );\n      if (violations.rows.length > 0) {\n        const sample = violations.rows\n          .slice(0, 5)\n          .map((r) => `${r.table_schema}.${r.table_name}`)\n          .join(', ');\n        throw new Error(\n          `B.5b platform-level schema \"${platformSchema}\" has ${violations.rows.length} ` +\n            `tenant_<uuid>.<table> clone(s) of its tables — first 5: ${sample}. ` +\n            `Platform schemas are global by definition; per-tenant clones violate ` +\n            `the trust-boundary model. Investigate which migration or provisioning ` +\n            `path created them and remove via DROP TABLE on the offending tenant ` +\n            `clones.`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n});\n","numLines":746,"startLine":1,"totalLines":746}}}

Imported once\n// per tenant-scoped service so TenantSchemaMiddleware and the TenantProvisioned\n// subscriber share one cache instance (no stale-negative-cache block for\n// freshly provisioned tenants).\nexport * from './tenant-schema-cache';\n\n// Tenant Schema Utilities (pure functions, no DI required)\nexport * from './tenant-schema.utils';\n\n// Fair, bounded per-tenant-schema cron/scheduler fan-out (cron-fairness).\n// Bounded concurrency + per-tenant Node+DB timeout + error isolation + rotation.\nexport * from './for-each-tenant-schema';\n\n// Migration Logger (structured logging for TypeORM migrations outside DI)\nexport { MigrationLogger } from './migration-logger';\n\n// Migration ledger SSoT. runtime services only need read access for SchemaVersionGate.\nexport {\n  buildTenantMigrationLedgerReadGrant,\n  grantTenantMigrationLedgerReadAccess,\n  serviceRoleForTenantAwareSchema,\n} from './tenant-migration-ledger-privileges';\nexport type {\n  TenantMigrationLedgerQueryExecutor,\n  TenantMigrationLedgerReadGrant,\n  TenantMigrationLedgerReadGrantOptions,\n} from './tenant-migration-ledger-privileges';\n// Tenant-schema table privilege SSoT (2026-07-06 grant incident): per-tenant\n// table clones must carry <source>_schema_owner ownership + <source>_service\n// DML regardless of which connection created them. assert = idempotent align;\n// verify = deploy/job-blocking drift detection.\nexport {\n  assertTenantSchemaPrivileges,\n  verifyTenantSchemaPrivileges,\n  ownerRoleForTenantAwareSchema,\n  serviceReadOnlyTenantTablesForSourceSchema,\n  tenantTablesForSourceSchema,\n} from './tenant-schema-privileges';\nexport type {\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeExecutor,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeOptions,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeReport,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeVerification,\n  TenantSchemaPrivilegeViolation,\n} from './tenant-schema-privileges';\nexport { grantTenantMessagingPartitionAuthority } from './messaging-partition-privileges';\nexport type {\n  MessagingPartitionAuthorityGrant,\n  MessagingPartitionAuthorityOptions,\n  MessagingPartitionAuthorityQueryExecutor,\n} from './messaging-partition-privileges';\n\n// Per-tenant sensor TimescaleDB rollup SSoT. db-migrate owns production DDL;\n// sensor runtime consumes the same names/SQL only for verification and local\n// non-authoritative development bootstrap.\nexport * from './sensor-continuous-aggregate-definition';\n\n// Migration helpers — column/table existence guards. Shared across all\n// services for migrations that reference state created by squashed\n// earlier migrations. See migration-helpers.ts docblock for rationale.\nexport { columnExists, tableExists } from './migration-helpers';\n\n// Transactional outbox DDL — one SQL shape for every service-local\n// outbox table that backs @platform/outbox. In dev\n// (default) delegates to the runner verbatim. Would have caught the 2026-04-14 audit_logs.tenantId drift\n// weeks before it broke RLS in production.
